### INT-483 · Use takes the thing before a villager beside it; Talk takes the villager; Next chooses among several (GP-ANDROID-USE-TALK-TARGETING, Expansion 20)
- **Trigger:** The targets on offer gathered each step (main._find_target, _find_target_below); Use (E, A, the Use button, a tap); Talk (T, Y while a villager is offered, a tap on them); Next (the prompt's button on touch), Q / Tab, the D-pad.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/main.gd::_find_target`, `game/scripts/main.gd::use_order`, `game/scripts/main.gd::_find_target_below`, `game/scripts/main.gd::cycle_target`, `game/scripts/main.gd::_next_hint`, `game/scripts/ui/game_ui.gd::prompt`
- **Affects:** Interface, Crafting, Workshops, Containers, Market
- **Expected:**
  - Use offers every thing in reach (station, chest, counter or desk, loot, seam, door, bed...) before any villager to talk to or animal (use_order: each group in its old order); a trader at their post keeps its place (it is the counter's use); with only people about Use talks to the nearest, as before. Theft stays last and yielding to a guard first.
  - The nearest villager is offered to Talk whenever Use is on a thing (the prompt's talk line: T, Y or Tap them, shown after a dot on the prompt's one line, INT-490); T with a villager chosen talks to them.
  - On touch the prompt has a Next button with more than one thing on offer, showing which is chosen ("2/3"; a tap on it never walks); keys and pads keep Q / Tab and the D-pad. No Use Nearby button.
  - Reach, ownership, leave, access and every refusal are unchanged: only the order of what was already on offer.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/x20_android_ui_test.gd`, `game/tests/start_test.gd`

### INT-484 · The usable things in reach glow round their silhouettes, the chosen one brightest (GP-INTERACTABLE-OUTLINES, Expansion 20)
- **Trigger:** Every frame (main._sync_outlines) from the things on offer (target_options) and the chosen target.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/view/target_outline.gd`, `game/scripts/main.gd::_sync_outlines`, `game/scripts/main.gd::outline_shape`, `game/scripts/main.gd::outline_cells`
- **Affects:** Interface, Crafting, Workshops, Containers, Market, Resources
- **Expected:**
  - A faint glow round each usable thing on offer and a bright, breathing one round the target Use takes, following the drawn shape: the map's tiles on the thing's own cells (no ground, path or roof layer) with the pixels of the cells beside them joined to them, never a full floor or wall tile; a market shelf by its sprite's pixels.
  - Covered: stations (oven, stove, forge, anvil, workbench, sawhorse, a home's oven), chests, crates and barrels, desks and counters, market stalls, seams and boulders, ripe plants, the player's bed, chest and chairs. Never: a theft, a station refused (someone else working it, or no leave), people and animals, anything while a window is open, while placing, gathering, or below the surface.
  - At most six outlines; each shape built once per set of tiles (cached) and drawn as one sprite, looked up again only when the things on offer change. Drawn over the floor and under the people. Presentation only.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/x20_android_ui_test.gd`

### INT-485 · The contextual prompt is for choosing: down while its interaction runs, gone when it is stale, back when the thing is chosen again (GP-INTERACTION-PROMPT-LIFECYCLE, Expansion 20)
- **Trigger:** Each step's prompt (main._prompt from _find_target and _find_target_below); Use (interact); a window open (the modal branch of _player_step); the player's own timed work (the action branch).
- **Source:** `game/scripts/main.gd::_prompt`, `game/scripts/main.gd::interact`, `game/scripts/main.gd::_player_step`, `game/scripts/main.gd::cycle_target`
- **Affects:** Interface, Crafting
- **Expected:**
  - No prompt while a window is open or while the player's work runs; the bar over the head names the work (Crafting, Mining, Picking...).
  - Once Use has opened the target's window (prompt_done), its prompt stays down after the window closes and after the work, while that target stays chosen; another target, or none, clears it, so no prompt outlives its target; the prompt returns when the thing is chosen again (Next, Q / Tab, the D-pad, stepping away and back). Use still works it meanwhile, and its outline stays bright.
  - Standing beside a thing never hides or holds its prompt by itself.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/x20_android_ui_test.gd`, `game/tests/start_test.gd`

### INT-490 · Messages and the prompt keep to the screen's edges; the messages worth keeping are saved in the world's journal (GP-MESSAGE-FOOTPRINT, GP-MESSAGE-JOURNAL, Expansion 20)
- **Trigger:** Any message (GameUI.message: the morning greeting, refusals, results, network notices) and the category its caller gives it; what a villager says in the talk window; each step's prompt (GameUI.prompt); a tap or click on the message; opening the journal; saving and loading.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/ui/game_ui.gd::message`, `game/scripts/ui/game_ui.gd::dismiss_message`, `game/scripts/ui/game_ui.gd::_talk_view`, `game/scripts/ui/game_ui.gd::game_messages`, `game/scripts/ui/game_ui.gd::_place_toast`, `game/scripts/ui/game_ui.gd::_push_log`, `game/scripts/ui/game_ui.gd::prompt`, `game/scripts/ui/game_ui.gd::_place_bottom`, `game/scripts/ui/game_ui.gd::show_journal`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::player_tell`, `game/scripts/core/message_journal.gd`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::c_journal`
- **Affects:** Interface, Save
- **Expected:**
  - The message is a small box (14 points, was 17) at the top edge, right of the status box and left of the touch columns, over the play centre where the room allows; it fades over its last 0.6 seconds and a tap or click on it puts it away (a tap on it never walks). The corner log steps below it while the two would overlap.
  - The prompt is one slim line (14 points, was 16; 44 units high, was 61) just above the bottom edge, centred in the room left of the touch columns and Recenter and right of the thumbstick: the target, then after a dot the talk line or, on keys and pads, the next choice; on touch the next choice is the small Next button showing which is chosen ("2/3"). prompt_text keeps its lines unchanged.
  - A message whose caller gives it a category of `game/data/message_journal.json` is kept by the World (World.player_tell) for the acting player, by the player's reference, as [world minutes, category, words]; an untagged message (routine feedback) is not kept; the same words straight after themselves the same day are kept once; dismissing or fading a message never removes its entry. What a villager says in the talk window is kept ("npc"), except the opening greeting and words the player's action already put there. On a guest's machine player_tell keeps the entry in its copy and hands it to the host over its own channel (NetSession.send_journal, c_journal), which keeps it in its world under the guest; it is not a player verb, never goes through World.mirror or the host's request queue, and so never repeats or adds to an action. The host's answers to a guest's requests (NetSession.answered) are shown and not kept; joins and leaves (NetSession.changed) are kept.
  - Two tiers per player: significant (crime, law, family, contract, work, quest, property, village, skill; cap 2000) and ordinary (trade, money, npc, find, game; cap 300); past a cap that tier's oldest entry goes, never the other tier's.
  - Saved in the world under the optional key "messages" (written only when not empty; no save version change): a save without it (Build 21 version 22, Build 22 version 23) loads with an empty journal and everything else as before. The journal's Messages page reads it, by day and time. The journal's pages (Messages, Village record, Quests) each hold what fits their height at the window's size, measured, never splitting an entry, with the page buttons in the window, so Earlier and Later page through and nothing scrolls; on a phone the page buttons stand above the pages, in their words' width.
  - Never read by the simulation and draws no random number: the world's state and the determinism gate are unchanged by it.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/message_journal_test.gd`, `game/tests/x20_android_ui_test.gd`, `game/tests/start_test.gd`

### INT-500 · A completed sale is observed by the Living Economy model, when it is on (Expansion 20 LE-01)
- **Trigger:** `World.trade` completes a sale while `game/data/economy.json` "enabled" is true (shipped false: `World.economy` is null and nothing happens).
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/world.gd::trade`, `game/scripts/core/economy/economy_foundation.gd::on_trade`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_events.gd::record_at`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_knowledge.gd::learn`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_needs.gd::mark`, `game/scripts/core/business_money.gd::till_ref_for`
- **Affects:** Market, Ledger, Households, Save
- **Expected:** After the goods and coins have moved (and only then), one "trade" event is recorded; the buyer learns that the seller sells the item at the price paid and the seller that the buyer buys it (knowledge source "dealing", one entry per knower, subject, item and side, refreshed, never duplicated; no trust or opinion stored: that stays with Judgment, `EconActor.judged_person` maps a ref to the person it judges); the two parties and their households are marked for their needs to be derived again; a sale whose proceeds go into a storefront's or trade counter's till (`BusinessMoney.till_ref_for`) names that till in the event's note ("till:front:<id>", "till:counter:<id>") and marks the till's controller. Nothing is moved, priced, refused or decided; no random number is drawn; no population scan. With the economy off, the game is identical (G0).
- **Tests:** `game/tests/economy_model_test.gd`, `game/tests/economy_foundation_test.gd`, `game/tests/till_ownership_test.gd`

### INT-501 · The Living Economy model's dawn: expiries, contract links, contract terms learned, needs derived (Expansion 20 LE-01)
- **Trigger:** `World._new_day` (06:00) while the economy is on; after the households' firewood.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/world.gd::_new_day`, `game/scripts/core/economy/economy_foundation.gd::dawn`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_offers.gd::expire_due`, `game/scripts/core/economy/reservations.gd::expire_due`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_contracts.gd::sweep`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_needs.gd::refresh`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_needs.gd::derive`, `game/scripts/core/contracts.gd::get_contract`
- **Affects:** Market, Households, Building, Employment, Save
- **Expected:** Offers past their expiry day expire (their reservations released), then reservations; input claims linked to a CON-01 contract are released once the contract module has ended it (lapse, cancel, death under OD-29: the contract decides, the model follows); the two parties of each contract new since the last dawn learn its item and price (source "contract", told once by a saved cursor); at most `needs.refresh_budget` dirty actors have their needs derived from existing state (hh.econ days of food against reserve_days, JOB_TOOL, NPCBrain.wants, contract shortfalls, the household's own site), sorted. Deriving calls no function that writes (no evaluate_household, no hourly cache). Records only; nothing in live play reads them. Saved in the optional "economy" key (needs, offers, knowledge, contracts, contract_cursor), each part only when non-empty; while disabled the section is carried unchanged (D2).
- **Tests:** `game/tests/economy_model_test.gd`, `game/tests/economy_sim.gd`

### INT-520 · A lie shown false is the liar's own deed, judged and passed on (X19-MISINFO, Expansion 20 R1)
- **Trigger:** A guard, or kin of the accused, hears an invented story and shows it false (`JudgmentStore.tell` -> `_refute`).
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::_refute`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::_refutation`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::_drop_lie`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::learn`, `game/data/judgment.json`
- **Affects:** Judgment, Crime
- **Expected:** The catalog's `false_accusation` (BAD, severity 1000, family information) is published once per lie: actor the liar, victims those the lie accused, tags `["refutes", lie]`, and the lie's tags gain the refutation's id (`["lie", liar, refutation]`). The one who showed it false and the teller who had believed it take it in firsthand (witnessed; the accused, if present, as participant) and judge the liar by the catalog's interpretation rules (affected, kin, friend, bystander, rival), like any deed. Their trust in the liar falls by the approved `trust.rumour_false` (15 points) only, not the catalog's `false_accusation.trust` (20) on top. It is then told like any deed; whoever learns it later and believed the lie drops the lie and the judgment it gave them (owner: "the negative judgment caused by that accusation is immediately corrected"), and their trust in the liar moves by the catalog's rule for a told deed (20 points times their confidence). A second refutation of the same lie reuses the fact; each further lie shown false is a further deed (repetition). The reference `gossip.timing` "hour" never invents, so never refutes. No item or coin is involved; no new number.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/judgment_x19_test.gd::test_refutation_fact`, `game/tests/judgment_x19_test.gd::test_lies`

### INT-521 · The flight recorder records judgment: facts, beliefs, gossip, ledger posts, trust and every weighted choice in full (REC-19 D5, REC-STORE, FAM-SAC-01; Expansion 20 R3)
- **Trigger:** With a recorder attached (off by default: `recorder.json` `enabled` false; `economy_sim.gd --record`, a test, or the desktop "Record gameplay" setting), any judgment record changing or any `JudgmentWeighting.choose`.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::publish`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::learn`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::tell`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::_post`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::trust_add`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_weighting.gd::choose`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_family.gd::_record`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_live.gd::attach`, `game/scripts/core/recorder/world_recorder.gd::judgment_choice`, `game/scripts/core/recorder/world_recorder.gd::attach`
- **Affects:** Recorder, Judgment, Family
- **Expected:** `JudgmentStore.rec` is null unless a recorder is attached (never saved). Then each fact is an event with its actors, victims and beneficiaries; each belief taken in is an event on the fact's thread with its ledger posts (`judgment_good` / `judgment_bad` of an actor for a fact, contribution before and after) and trust moves as state rows; a telling that passed something on is an event whose children are the beliefs it gave (one with nothing to tell writes nothing); every weighted choice is a decision with every candidate as an alternative (infeasible ones with the live code's why), and every factor value, weighted contribution, family colour and stubborn penalty as factor rows, with forgo, never sampled (at the storage cap the session stops, keeps everything and is marked incomplete, REC-D2); each family-priority decision is an event with the rule's values, linked to its weighing. The recorder is handed values the store already computed: it calls no store function, draws no number and changes no decision, so a run with and without it is identical (fingerprint, RNG, judgment state hash).
- **Tests:** `game/tests/recorder_test.gd::test_judgment_and_relations`, `game/tests/recorder_test.gd::test_disabled`, `game/tests/recorder_test.gd::test_coverage_file`

### INT-522 · The flight recorder records the relations' shared hours, gifts and harm (REC-19 D4; Expansion 20 R3)
- **Trigger:** With a recorder attached, `Relations.share` (each pair's shared hour), `Relations.give` (a gift) and `Relations.harmed` (harm and its spread to kin and friends).
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/relations.gd::share`, `game/scripts/core/relations.gd::give`, `game/scripts/core/relations.gd::harmed`, `game/scripts/core/recorder/world_recorder.gd::shared_hour`, `game/scripts/core/recorder/world_recorder.gd::gift_end`, `game/scripts/core/recorder/world_recorder.gd::harm_tie`
- **Affects:** Recorder, Family, Inventory
- **Expected:** Each is one event on the pair's tie (`tie:<a>:<b>`) with regard, attraction and days known before and after; a gift also records the item leaving the giver and reaching the receiver (net zero); a harm records every tie it lowered. With no recorder the code paths are unchanged (the shared hour's early return became the same condition the other way round). Help, talk, cooling, courting at dawn and proposals are recorded since X20-FINAL (INT-570).
- **Tests:** `game/tests/recorder_test.gd::test_judgment_and_relations`

### INT-523 · A villager who knows of a crime weighs telling a guard against keeping quiet (SJD-F crime hook; Expansion 20 R2; OFF by default)
- **Trigger:** `World.report_crime` (a witness of an attack or a theft, its victim, or someone who sights a wanted player) while `judgment.json` `decision.integration.report` is true. It ships false: until R6 measures it, every call reports as before.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/world.gd::report_crime`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_live.gd::will_report`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_weighting.gd::choose`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_family.gd::_shield`, `game/data/judgment.json`
- **Affects:** Crime, Judgment
- **Expected:** Two feasible options through the one shared interface (`JudgmentWeighting.choose`, profile `report`): telling (the live rule's yes as `live_order`, and the harm: the victim, a witness of an attack, a witness of a theft) and keeping quiet (the villager's own opinion of the offender with the family name's colour, kinship with the offender, fear from the violence they hold against them). Each person's weights spread by personality (trait spread, life shift, stubbornness: keeping quiet for someone resented is penalised), so two witnesses of the same crime may decide differently; there is no reputation cutoff and no guaranteed outcome (owner, 2026-10-26). Who may report, and how a report travels and is delivered (PAR-05: guards, `report.range`, `report.hours`, `World.deliver_report`), are unchanged; a report made is the same report as with the hook off. One who keeps quiet still holds the memory of what they saw (the judgment fact is published before the choice). A family member fetching help against a danger (`JudgmentFamily._shield`) is a rule, never weighed. Every choice is recorded by the flight recorder when one is attached (INT-521). Politics stays out (Expansion 21). Numbers are PROVISIONAL agent values (`decision.report`, profile weights).
- **Tests:** `game/tests/judgment_live_test.gd::test_r2_report_off`, `game/tests/judgment_live_test.gd::test_r2_report_on`

### INT-524 · A relative's gift toward a house becomes the family's history, handed down and told by descendants (GM-01, WDR-009-C; Expansion 20 R4)
- **Trigger:** `Construction._family_help` gives coins toward a household's house (judgment attached: on by default); the house is finished (`World._finish_site`); a parent and a newly adult child are at home together (`JudgmentLive.tell_family_tick`); the player opens a conversation (`Dialogue.opening`).
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/construction.gd::_family_help`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_live.gd::on_build_help`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_live.gd::on_built`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_live.gd::tell_family_tick`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::set_refs`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::make_history`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::significant`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::building_done`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::tell_family_history`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::_finish_site`, `game/scripts/core/event_talk.gd::history`, `game/scripts/core/event_talk.gd::house_state`, `game/scripts/ui/dialogue_voice.gd::history_line`, `game/scripts/ui/dialogue.gd::opening`, `game/data/judgment.json`, `game/data/dialogue_events.json`
- **Affects:** Judgment, Building, Family, Property, Save, Interface
- **Expected:** The `help_build` fact is published exactly as before and gains entity references (the household helped, the giving household, the plot, the amount, the house's cost still to pay) and a link `["contributed", "plot:<id>"]`. By the significance rule (`judgment.json` `history`, PROVISIONAL: at least `min_value` 10 coins and `significant_share_permille` 200 of that cost) it becomes a historical record, known firsthand by the members of both households; when the house is finished the fact and record name the building and gain `["built", "building:<id>"]`. The fact retires with the memory horizon; the record stays. A parent at home with a new adult child hands down every record they know, up to `max_generations` tellings from the deed. In conversation, a villager who knows a record that touches the listener's line (`EventTalk.generations`, Life's family graph, villagers and players alike) or their own family tells it once to that listener, worded from the record, the lineage and the house as it stands now: still the family's, the listener's, sold (who has it now) or gone; never from fixed text. Knowledge only: no ledger, trust, gossip or decision reads it, and no random number is drawn, so the simulation is unchanged (G0 identical). Saved in the judgment section (version 3, optional keys `refs` and `history`); a version 1-2 section loads with none; `save_version` is unchanged. Coin gifts only: no game system yet gives a household materials toward another's house, and nothing pulls a house down (the destroyed state reads a building that no longer exists).
- **Tests:** `game/tests/history_test.gd::test_three_generations`, `game/tests/history_test.gd::test_significance`, `game/tests/history_test.gd::test_old_save`, `game/tests/judgment_x19_test.gd::test_save_and_upgrade`

### INT-525 · A villager's aspirations weigh in their yes-or-forgo choices (R5, WDR-011-C; Expansion 20; OFF by default)
- **Trigger:** `judgment.json` `aspiration.enabled` true (ships false). Each dawn, between the households' evaluation and the search for work (`NPCBrain.dawn`), every adult villager's six domains are updated; then every `JudgmentWeighting.choose` under `work_accept`, `contract_accept`, `credit_lend`, `barter_accept` or `family_help`.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_aspiration.gd::dawn`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_aspiration.gd::satisfaction`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_aspiration.gd::factor`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_weighting.gd::_choose`, `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::dawn`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_live.gd::work_refusal`, `game/data/judgment.json`
- **Affects:** Judgment, Employment, Households, Property, Family, Coins
- **Expected:** Six domains (food, wealth, property, profession, family, standing) each have a priority (a per-person temperament hash, age, and bounded saved shifts), a satisfaction read from existing state only (household food days and streak, hunger, purse and income, homes owned or leased, farmland, housing goals, tools, job and skills, partner and children, others' personal judgments) and a pressure; the highest pressure leads, or the villager is content. In the five profiles one factor, `aspiration`, is 50 points plus the leading domain's fit (-1, 0, +1 from `aspiration.fit`) times its pressure (capped), and doing without is credited the same 50 points at the person's weight, so a neutral aspiration changes no answer. Only candidates the live code found feasible are weighed; nothing creates, moves or destroys a coin, a food point or an item, and a villager's ambitions may stay unmet (WDR-022). The family-first profiles (`family_*`) gain no factor and no survival pressure (FAMILY-01 unchanged). Off: no factor, no update, no saved key; G0 identical.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/aspiration_test.gd::test_v2_hungry_parent_protects`, `game/tests/aspiration_test.gd::test_v9_disabled_is_baseline`, `game/tests/aspiration_test.gd::test_v10_no_resources`, `game/tests/aspiration_test.gd::test_domains_and_neutral`

### INT-526 · Severe hunger, injury and danger press on every aspiration, and ease after recovery (R5, FAD Q1; OFF by default)
- **Trigger:** With aspirations on: a villager's hunger between `aspiration.hunger_from` (35, well below the ordinary appetite) and the owner's survival floor (25), health between `aspiration.injury_from` and `decision.family.danger.hp_floor_permille`, or real harm to them or their household within `danger_days` (an assault or killing: `danger_step`; a theft or pocket picked: `theft_step`), read from the facts' victims; what they have heard about other people never counts.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_aspiration.gd::raw_survival`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_aspiration.gd::survival`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_aspiration.gd::eff_priority`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_aspiration.gd::yield_of`
- **Affects:** Judgment, Food, Health, Combat, Family
- **Expected:** The survival level (0..1000) raises food's priority and makes the other five yield by their `yield_base`, softened by stubbornness (a stubborn villager holds on longer) and never below `yield_floor`; no domain is cancelled. It rises at once at a decision and falls at most `recover_per_day` a dawn, so earlier ambitions regain influence gradually; it is not a priority shift. It never enters the family-first decisions: a hungry parent still gives way to hungrier children, the survival floor still makes them eat, and a head under the health floor may still flee.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/aspiration_test.gd::test_v1_survival`, `game/tests/aspiration_test.gd::test_v1_danger_is_real_harm`, `game/tests/aspiration_test.gd::test_v2_hungry_parent_protects`, `game/tests/aspiration_test.gd::test_v3_no_needless_sacrifice`

### INT-527 · Career ambition weighs a job change against hardship (R5, FAD Q2, job_choice; OFF by default)
- **Trigger:** `NPCBrain.seek_work` for a villager looking for work, with `decision.integration.job_choice` and `aspiration.enabled` both true (both ship false; the job flag alone is refused with a logged reason).
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::seek_work`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_live.gd::pick_job`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_live.gd::on`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_aspiration.gd::skill_fit`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_aspiration.gd::growth_fit`
- **Affects:** Employment, Wages, Judgment, Households
- **Expected:** Who looks for work, which jobs are open, the tool's price and the need for training are the live rule's. Among the feasible options and staying, the profile `job_choice` weighs the live rule's order, the pay, the pay gain magnified by hardship (coin need and the household's unsustainable days), skill and room to improve (scaled by the person's profession priority, which survival pressure lowers), and tenure and career satisfaction for staying. A skilled villager may stay rather than take slightly better pay; severe hardship still makes the switch attractive. With equal career factors the live answer stands; the manor's hiring competition (WDR-012-C) still feeds the pay. Off: `seek_work` is the live rule exactly.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/aspiration_test.gd::test_v4_job_choice`

### INT-528 · Romantic preferences and social standing (R5, FAD Q3 and Q6; OFF by default)
- **Trigger:** `Relations._villagers_court` → `JudgmentLive.pick_court` (integration `court_choice`, on) with aspirations on; a villager's priority shifts at a success or setback, a bereavement (`JudgmentLive.on_death`) or a marriage (`JudgmentLive.on_marriage`).
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_live.gd::pick_court`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_live.gd::court_factors`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_aspiration.gd::standing_value`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_aspiration.gd::setback`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_aspiration.gd::on_loss`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_aspiration.gd::on_marriage`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::to_dict`, `game/scripts/core/recorder/world_recorder.gd::aspiration_event`
- **Affects:** Family, Judgment, Households, Property, Save, Recorder
- **Expected:** Whom a single villager courts weighs attraction, their own opinion of the candidate, the bond they have (regard, days known), the candidate household's security, relative wealth, property, stability, the chooser's opinions of the candidate's parents and siblings, a shared leading ambition and the candidate's standing, each scaled by the chooser's own priorities and per-person spread, so villagers prefer differently; nothing gates (no wealth requirement), and the pool, kinship, ages, consent and proposal acceptance are the live rules' (WDR-011-Q8 open); no NPC decides for a player. Standing is the mean of other villagers' personal judgments plus recognition; family colour never enters it; in a courting decision the chooser's own entry is left out, so no ledger entry counts twice. Setbacks: a holder of stubbornness perseveres, others adapt (faster with wisdom), three in a game year change even a stubborn mind. Saved in the judgment section's optional `aspire` key (version 4 only when present; columns by domain name, unknown ones carried); `save_version` unchanged. Each shift, lead change and survival step is a recorder event `judgment/aspiration`.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/aspiration_test.gd::test_v5_romance`, `game/tests/aspiration_test.gd::test_v6_perseverance`, `game/tests/aspiration_test.gd::test_v7_standing`, `game/tests/aspiration_test.gd::test_v8_save_load`, `game/tests/aspiration_test.gd::test_recorder`

### INT-529 · A villager's own preferences decide whether they accept an eligible proposal (WDR-011-Q8 answer A; OFF by default)
- **Trigger:** `Relations.propose` by a villager suitor (from `Relations._villagers_court`) or a player (the talk window's Propose, `GameUI`) to a villager, with `aspiration.enabled` true, after every hard limit has passed.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/relations.gd::propose`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_live.gd::accepts_proposal`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_live.gd::court_factors`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_weighting.gd::_choose`, `game/scripts/core/guardianship.gd::player_household`, `game/data/judgment.json`
- **Affects:** Family, Judgment, Households
- **Expected:** Courting, `can_court` (kinship, age, existing partners), `refusal` (willingness, notoriety, regard, a rival) and the too-soon gate come first and are never weighed. An eligible proposal is then weighed by the one shared interface (profile `proposal_accept`, the courting factors and weights scaled by the villager's own priorities, no ledger entry counted twice) against doing without; the same proposal may be accepted by one villager and refused by another. A refusal by preference ends that courtship as a rival does. No financial cutoff. A player's proposal to a villager is weighed the same way (PROP-EQ, D39 follow-up under WDR-012; before, the live rule answered it), the player read in their own form by the same `court_factors`: the household `Guardianship.player_household` (a couple household; a single player has none and is read as a villager without a household is), wealth the player's purse, no trade (the game records none for a player, so no tenure part of stability), family compatibility through the player's own mother and father and their children, standing everyone else's opinion of the player's person id, no aspiration row (property 0, shared ambition neutral). Only the villager's own answer is weighed: a player's consent is never decided by an NPC, and a proposal between two players stays their own consent offer (INT-561). Off: the live rule exactly, for villager and player suitors alike.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/aspiration_test.gd::test_q8_proposals`, `game/tests/aspiration_test.gd::test_q8_player_proposals`
### INT-560 · Another player in reach is chosen like a villager: prompt, ring, Next, Use, Talk, a tap or a click (MP-INTERACT-01, Expansion 20)
- **Trigger:** Each step's targets (main._find_target, _find_target_below) in a session; a tap or left click on another player's drawn character (main._tap, _tap_below); Use (E, A, the Use button), Talk (T, Y), Next (Q / Tab, the D-pad, the prompt's Next).
- **Source:** `game/scripts/main.gd::other_players`, `game/scripts/main.gd::_player_targets`, `game/scripts/main.gd::_player_at`, `game/scripts/main.gd::_talk_player_step`, `game/scripts/main.gd::open_player`, `game/scripts/main.gd::use_order`, `game/scripts/main.gd::_sync_player_marks`, `game/scripts/view/player_mark.gd`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::s_players`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::announce`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::s_public`
- **Affects:** Interface, Player state
- **Expected:**
  - Each other player in play (on the host the connected guests; on a guest those the host reports), alive, not away, on the player's level, within World.OFFER_REACH and with a clear line, whose feet are within the probe's reach in front, is a target of kind "player" named "<name> (player)" by its pid (the stable identity); never oneself. Ordered with the villagers by distance, then by pid.
  - Use takes things before people (INT-483: "player" is a character kind); Talk (talk_alt, T, the pad's Y) takes the nearest person, a player included; Next reaches each player separately; the choice holds while they move in reach.
  - A tap or left click on a player's drawn character (nearer the tap than any villager or animal there) walks up to them, following, and opens their window; attack mode and right-click strike as before (players are not struck by a click). A click on open ground still walks.
  - A faint ring at each player on offer, bright round the chosen one (PlayerMark, drawn at the avatar's place, under the people); none while a window is open, placing or gathering.
  - A guest's copy follows each other player's level and presence (s_players carries the level; those the host no longer reports are away); a guest who joins later, or an heir, is announced to the others (s_public, their public record only). Another level's players are not drawn.
  - Single player: no other players, so nothing changes (net.role empty).
- **Tests:** `game/tests/mp_interact_ui_test.gd`, `game/tests/net_interact_test.gd`

### INT-561 · Between two players every interaction is a consent offer the other answers in the player's window (MP-INTERACT-01, MP-D8, Expansion 20)
- **Trigger:** The player window (PlayerWindow.show, GameUI.show_player; the list GameUI.show_players, from Use with nothing else in reach and the pause menu's Players); World.player_offer and World.player_answer, on a guest sent to the host (World.mirror; player_answer without a local run).
- **Source:** `game/scripts/ui/player_window.gd`, `game/scripts/ui/game_ui.gd::show_player`, `game/scripts/ui/game_ui.gd::show_players`, `game/scripts/ui/game_ui.gd::show_pause`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::player_offer`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::player_answer`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::offers_to`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::messages_to`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::offer_text`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::player_contract_deliver`, `game/scripts/core/contracts.gd::open_between`, `game/scripts/core/contracts.gd::face_to_face`, `game/scripts/main.gd::_tell_offers`, `game/scripts/net/net_sync.gd::adopt`
- **Affects:** Interface, Inventory, Coins, Market, Family, Save, Player state
- **Expected:**
  - Kinds on the MP-D8 record: partner, gift, sell (goods for a price in all), buy (a bid for the other's goods), contract (the buyer asks the other to supply goods at a price each within days), supply (the seller offers to), say (a message of at most World.SAY_MAX characters, one line), note (the host tells the offerer the answer; never offered by a player). sell/buy and contract/supply are each other's Counter.
  - Offering needs the two beside each other (OFFER_REACH, one level, both in play); the offerer's own goods or coins are checked; nothing of the other's is touched. One offer of a kind per pair; at most MESSAGES_KEPT messages and notes wait for one player.
  - Accepting rechecks everything: what cannot be done now (not beside them, no room, coins short) waits; what can never be (their goods gone, lapsed, gone from play) is removed and the offerer told. A sale or bid is World.trade (goods and coins together or nothing, the LE-01 and relations hooks as any trade), stolen marks moving with the goods; a contract opens by Contracts.open_between with CON-01's rules (coins set aside, paid per unit delivered, partial, either may end it, lapse at dawn, formal ones face to face, each side's limit); the seller delivers beside the buyer (player_contract_deliver). Declining moves nothing.
  - The recipient is told once in the compact message (and the journal's Messages page, category "players", "trade", "contract" or "family"); the offerer gets a note with the answer. Offers, messages and notes lapse at the day's end and are saved (optional fields price, days, text). The world never waits.
  - Messages are never read by villagers, written to the village record or shown as anyone else's words.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/mp_interact_ui_test.gd`, `game/tests/net_interact_test.gd`, `game/tests/multiplayer_c_test.gd`

### INT-562 · A guest's requests: free text is text, a direction is not a place, and the adit is asked for only where it is (MP-INTERACT-01, Expansion 20)
- **Trigger:** NetActions.apply_verb on the host for a guest's request; World.player_through on a guest's machine.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/net/net_actions.gd::apply_verb`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::player_through`
- **Affects:** Interface, Player state
- **Expected:**
  - The arguments a player wrote or was shown (player_offer's text, player_tell's words) are passed as plain text, never decoded: a message reading "player" is not the host's reference. Every other argument is decoded and checked as before (another player's reference, person id, character or store refused).
  - player_through's argument is a direction: it is exempt from the 24-tile place check (the verb works from the guest's own place on the host). A guest's machine sends it only when its copy of the map has a passage there, not on every walking step.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/net_interact_test.gd`, `game/tests/mp_interact_ui_test.gd`

### INT-563 · A purchase is chosen on the price the act pays, and a failed one is not chosen again unchanged (SIM-F2, owner D17, Expansion 20)
- **Trigger:** A villager's decision plans a `buy_from` trip: a dresser's mallet, iron chisel or stone (`NPCBrain._fetch_dress_tool`, `NPCBrain._fetch_dress_stone`), a worker's replacement tool (`NPCBrain._buy_tool`), medicine (`NPCBrain._plan_heal`), a bandit's food at the market (`NPCBrain._bandit_market`), cart, plough and minecart parts (`NPCBrain._parts_trip`), a whole handcart (`NPCBrain._plan_cart`), the household's firewood and grain (`NPCBrain._plan_provision`); a `buy_from` act fails.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::buy_open`, `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::buy_qty`, `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::buy_held`, `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::_parts_trip`, `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::_plan_cart`, `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::_plan_provision`, `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::act`, `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::_record_refused_buy`, `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::_fetch_dress_tool`, `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::_fetch_dress_stone`, `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::_buy_tool`, `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::_bandit_market`
- **Affects:** Market, Employment, Bandits, Recorder
- **Expected:** The trip is chosen only when the act would make the purchase: the seller holds the units and the buyer can pay the bargained price (`World.deal_price`, the list price moved by both sides' Trading) by the act's own rule (`World.can_afford` above the reserve; a necessity, the household's pantry or the trade's own tool from the whole purse). A parts trip (the parts counted together) is planned only when the act would sell every part at that price with the caller's floor left, as before on the list price; firewood and grain, which the act may sell in part, are planned in the quantity it would sell at that price (`NPCBrain.buy_qty`), and not at all when that is none; the fence and seed purchases keep FarmPlots' once-a-day limit. Before, the list price let a purse between the two prices plan a purchase the act refused, and the empty plan chose it again at once, all day (recorder run 1: 328 failed chisel purchases; seed 1 at 87b69ab: 80 on day 2 and 56 on day 5 by two labourers, then 364 axe purchases by a woodcutter). A `buy_from` that fails anyway is remembered (`NPCBrain.buy_failed`: seller, item, bargained price, the seller's stock, the purse, the time; not saved, as a plan is not) and that purchase is not chosen again while the price, the stock and the purse are unchanged, for at most `BUY_RETRY_MINUTES` (60, PROVISIONAL). A refusal the act made before `World.trade` was asked is a recorder trade event (`refused`, reason `refused_by_rules` with detail `below_reserve`, or `seller_lacks_item`) with the figures. Nothing is bought on credit, no price changes, no coin or item moves differently: the act and `World.trade` are unchanged.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/sim_f2_test.gd`

### INT-564 · A bandit's stalk is a lasting watch, re-decided only when what it watches changes (SIM-F3, owner D18, Expansion 20)
- **Trigger:** A bandit at night finds prey (`NPCBrain._bandit`, `NPCBrain._prey_for`); a bandit's chase is given up blocked (`NPCBrain.fight`); a `watch` plan step runs (`World._run_plan`).
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::_bandit`, `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::watch_over`, `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::fight`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::_run_plan`
- **Affects:** Bandits, Combat
- **Expected:** A chase starts only where `fight` runs it: the prey within `CHASE_RANGE`, and not the same prey standing in the same cell where the bandit's last chase of it was given up blocked (`NPCBrain.stalk_lost`, not saved, as the chase target is not; cleared by a strike). Otherwise the bandit, shown stalking, holds a `watch` step on that prey, deciding nothing, until the night ends, `STALK_WATCH_MINUTES` (30, PROVISIONAL) pass (the prey is then chosen afresh), the bandit is hurt enough to rest, the prey is gone (dead, indoors, another level, away, too poor to rob), or what it waits for happens: the prey comes within range, or (after a blocked chase) leaves its cell. Before, the stalk set only the target with an empty plan: `fight` dropped an out-of-range target on the next tick and a blocked chase sent the bandit back to the camp to choose the same prey again, so the brain decided again and again (recorder run 1: 400+ empty decisions a day; seed 1 at 87b69ab with SIM-F2: 30-75 a day a bandit, after: 0-10). What bandits do is unchanged: the same prey rule, chase, robbery, retreat and give-ups; no new combat or faction mechanic. Outcomes move only through timing (seeds 1-3, 8 days: bandit deaths 0/2/3 before, 3/0/3 after).
- **Tests:** `game/tests/sim_f3_test.gd`

### INT-570 · The flight recorder records the Expansions 18-20 systems it missed: flows, contracts, credit, relations' other channels, memory over time, daily summaries and multiplayer (X20-FINAL phase 3)
- **Trigger:** With a recorder attached (off by default; `economy_sim.gd --record`, a probe or test, the desktop setting, or `MML_RECORD=DIR` for a whole process): `World.stat`, the contract and credit choke points, `Relations.helped`, `talked`, `dawn`, `_villagers_court`, `ask_court`, `propose`, `JudgmentStore._forget`, `make_history`, `tell_family_history`, `JudgmentPersonality._log`, `JudgmentBonds.on_belief`, the hour boundary, and the `NetSession` handshake, disconnect, request, sync and reply points.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/world.gd::stat`, `game/scripts/core/contracts.gd::_open`, `game/scripts/core/contracts.gd::deliver`, `game/scripts/core/contracts.gd::_pay_units`, `game/scripts/core/contracts.gd::_end`, `game/scripts/core/credit.gd::sell_on_credit`, `game/scripts/core/credit.gd::repay`, `game/scripts/core/credit.gd::_default`, `game/scripts/core/credit.gd::barter`, `game/scripts/core/relations.gd::helped`, `game/scripts/core/relations.gd::talked`, `game/scripts/core/relations.gd::dawn`, `game/scripts/core/relations.gd::ask_court`, `game/scripts/core/relations.gd::propose`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::_forget`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::make_history`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_personality.gd::_log`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_bonds.gd::on_belief`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::apply_requests`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::send_syncs`, `game/scripts/core/recorder/world_recorder.gd::stat_moved`, `game/scripts/core/recorder/world_recorder.gd::net_state_maybe`
- **Affects:** Recorder, Judgment, Family, Inventory
- **Expected:** Each site makes one guarded call with values it has already computed; `ask_court` and `propose` are wrappers that call the unchanged bodies (`_ask_court`, `_propose`) between the recorder's begin and end, and with no recorder (or on a guest, whose verbs go to the host) call the body alone. Production, consumption and unmet-need counters moved inside an open event become state rows under it; contracts and debts are object threads (`contract:<id>`, `debt:<id>`) with their coins and goods; the day's summaries read plain fields and pure functions only (judgment.stats, never `counts()`); a network digest hashes `World.to_dict` sections (`NetSync.digest`). Nothing draws a number or changes state: the same steps with and without the recorder leave `to_dict`, the judgment store's hash and the RNG identical.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/recorder_test.gd::test_x20_phase3`, `game/tests/recorder_test.gd::test_judgment_and_relations`, `tests/test_sim_gate.py`

### INT-571 · The flight recorder records business tills, ownership, migration, deaths' context and the day's economy and settlements (REC-DIAG)
- **Trigger:** With a recorder attached: a storefront, counter or stall till's coins move (`BusinessMoney.after_trade`, `_settle`, `Stalls.after_trade`, `settle`), an owner or tenant changes (`World.set_owner`, `set_tenant`), a built stall's holder is gone (`Stalls.daily`), people move between the village and a settlement (`Migration._book`), a villager or a settlement member dies (`World.kill`, `OffMapSettlement._died` through `World.offmap_person` and `Identity.move`), and the first hour of each day (`WorldRecorder._day_summaries`).
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/business_money.gd::after_trade`, `game/scripts/core/business_money.gd::_settle`, `game/scripts/core/stalls.gd::after_trade`, `game/scripts/core/stalls.gd::settle`, `game/scripts/core/stalls.gd::daily`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::set_owner`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::set_tenant`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::offmap_person`, `game/scripts/core/identity.gd::move`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/migration.gd::_book`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/offmap.gd::_died`, `game/scripts/core/recorder/world_recorder.gd::till_flow`, `game/scripts/core/recorder/world_recorder.gd::ownership`, `game/scripts/core/recorder/world_recorder.gd::_economy_day`, `game/scripts/core/recorder/world_recorder.gd::_settlement_day`, `tools/recorder_archive.py`
- **Affects:** Recorder, Coins, Market, Property, Households
- **Expected:** Each site makes one guarded call after the change with values already computed; with no recorder nothing runs. A till move is one event with both sides' coin rows (so the hourly audit attributes it) and the till's controller; an ownership change one event; the day's economy (food on offer and in store, each household's purse, food and members, each till's coins and owner) and the settlements (people, the hungry, coins, food, deaths) one event each a day; a death carries what the dead and their household held and the food on offer; a settlement member's death names its cause. Recording changes nothing (the same steps with and without a recorder end in the same world); the archive tool reports abnormal-run evidence for review, never a failure (WDR-022).
- **Tests:** `game/tests/recorder_test.gd::test_rec_diag`, `tests/test_sim_gate.py`

### INT-580 · Two villagers who meet see each other's offers and may bargain; a deal settles through World.trade (Expansion 20 LE-02)
- **Trigger:** `Relations.share` (two villagers spend an hour within the meeting radius) while the economy is on and `market.drive` is true.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/relations.gd::share`, `game/scripts/core/economy/economy_foundation.gd::on_encounter`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::on_encounter`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::negotiate`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::_settle`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_offers.gd::record_fill`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::trade`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_weighting.gd::choose`
- **Affects:** Market, Coins, Inventory, Judgment, Ledger
- **Expected:** Nothing when neither has an open offer (two index reads). Otherwise each learns the other's open offers (knowledge source in_person: OD-LE-02B D01, offers local to encounters, stalls and dialogue; no offer board), and the lower id's first bid for something the other asks for or holds for sale is bargained once: buyer's ceiling (need, urgency, alternatives, a contract's own price) and seller's floor (recipe cost, known better bids, household need, friend's price, scarcity), inside the band value x [price_min, price_max]; at most `max_rounds` concessions, each side's share spread by personality and urgency; no agreement is a valid end. Both then weigh the terms in `JudgmentWeighting.choose` (`market_buy`, `market_sell`: need, price, alternatives, surplus and their opinion of the other; doing without is valid; no cutoff). Settlement re-checks stock, the buyer's coins net of its reservations and OD-19 reserve, and both offers' remaining units, then `World.trade` (all or nothing), then `record_fill` on each offer (never beyond what remains), then a deal record. Coins and goods conserved; nothing books unless the trade happened.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/le02_market_test.gd::test_shortage_three_actors`, `game/tests/le02_market_test.gd::test_negative_paths`, `game/tests/le02_market_test.gd::test_partial_and_duplicate_fills`, `game/tests/le02_market_test.gd::test_opinion_weighed`

### INT-581 · Prices pass by word of mouth on the gossip's own meetings (Expansion 20 LE-02, OD-LE-10 D09)
- **Trigger:** `JudgmentStore._meet_now` (a queued or scheduled gossip meeting) while the economy is on and `market.drive` is true.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::_meet_now`, `game/scripts/core/judgment/judgment_store.gd::would_tell`, `game/scripts/core/economy/economy_foundation.gd::on_meeting`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::on_meeting`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_knowledge.gd::learn`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_knowledge.gd::reliability`
- **Affects:** Market, Judgment, Save
- **Expected:** When the gossip's own rule would let the teller tell the listener anything (`would_tell`: net judgment above the share threshold), each tells the other up to `word_of_mouth_per_meeting` prices it learned firsthand (own sight, dealing, contract, in person; freshest first) that the listener lacks or holds older: source word_of_mouth, `via` the teller, the teller's seen_day kept (age). Hearsay is not retold; older news never replaces newer; hearsay is bounded per villager (`knowledge.max_hearsay`, oldest forgotten). Reliability is computed when read: firsthand 1, word of mouth the listener's `JudgmentStore.credibility` of the teller (the gossip's own measure), times a half-life of age; nothing stores trust. No notice board, no separate rumour engine; nothing moves.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/le02_market_test.gd::test_knowledge_local_and_word_of_mouth`, `game/tests/economy_model_test.gd::test_knowledge`

### INT-582 · The market's dawn: offers reviewed, bids from needs, asks from stock, direct dialogue opens CON-01 contracts (Expansion 20 LE-02, OD-LE-02, OD-LE-03 D02)
- **Trigger:** `World._new_day` -> `EconomyFoundation.dawn`, after the model's own dawn, while the economy is on and `market.drive` is true.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::dawn`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::review`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::post_bids`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::post_asks`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::dialogues`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::procure`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_offers.gd::revise`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_offers.gd::commit`, `game/scripts/core/contracts.gd::propose`
- **Affects:** Market, Coins, Households, Employment, Judgment, Save
- **Expected:** Offers reviewed at most every `review_days`: a bid whose need is met is withdrawn, one no longer covered cut or withdrawn, an unmet one raised by `revise_share` of the gap to its own ceiling at most `max_revisions` times (never a fixed raise, never past the ceiling or the band); an unsold ask lowered toward its floor, one under a known bid raised toward it; goods or coins gone by other means cut the offer. Up to `posts_per_dawn` need-table actors (round robin) bid for their contract, tool, input and housing needs (food stays with the existing household rule), only what their spendable coins cover; up to `ask_sellers_per_dawn` traders post asks for what they hold for sale. Up to `dialogues_per_dawn` bids at least a day old are taken by their buyer to a seller it knows (its own knowledge), chosen through `JudgmentWeighting.choose` with doing without; a CON-01 procurement contract is proposed for the bid's units at its price per unit, any tradable product, the seller answering by CON-01's own evaluation and counters met inside the buyer's ceiling and cash within `max_rounds`; the units are committed on the bid (not filled), the bid's coin reservation lifted while CON-01 escrows the coins. Goods never move at a distance: the seller delivers by CON-01. Nothing moves otherwise.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/le02_market_test.gd::test_review_adaptive`, `game/tests/le02_market_test.gd::test_dawn_driver`, `game/tests/le02_market_test.gd::test_procurement_contract`, `game/tests/le02_market_test.gd::test_determinism`, `game/tests/le02_market_test.gd::test_save_mid_negotiation`

### INT-583 · A villager contract renegotiated on its last day: extension, changed payment, partial delivery or cancellation (Expansion 20 LE-02, OD-LE-13)
- **Trigger:** The market's dawn (INT-582), for each open goods contract between two villagers due that day with goods still owed beyond what the seller holds.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::renegotiate`, `game/scripts/core/contracts.gd::amend`, `game/scripts/core/contracts.gd::cancel`, `game/scripts/core/contracts.gd::means`, `game/scripts/core/recorder/world_recorder.gd::contract_amended`
- **Affects:** Market, Coins, Employment, Ledger
- **Expected:** A buyer that no longer needs the goods cancels (CON-01 cancel: no fee). Otherwise the seller puts what its circumstances allow: more days when its work (mine, fell, craft, harvest) can manage the rest within `max_extend_days`; a higher price when it must buy the goods dearer than the contract pays (inside the band, the buyer's ceiling and cash); else, having delivered or holding some, the quantity cut to delivered plus held with a day to bring it. The buyer weighs it (`market_buy`) and `Contracts.amend` records it: escrow = (qty - delivered) x price_each, the difference returned to or set aside from the buyer (fund_refusal), history "amend" with the old terms. No penalty is added; refused or unproposed contracts lapse by CON-01's own rule. Coins conserved. `Contracts.amend` is called by nothing else (the CON-01 rule "no renegotiation after acceptance" stands with the economy off).
- **Tests:** `game/tests/le02_market_test.gd::test_renegotiation`

### INT-600 · Every village sale is validated against the claims on both parties before anything moves (Expansion 20 LE-03, one transaction path)
- **Trigger:** `World.trade` (every villager, player, guest, feast, seed and market sale) while the economy is on.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/world.gd::trade`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_claims.gd::check_trade`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_claims.gd::plan`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_claims.gd::apply`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::_settle`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_offers.gd::_rebook`
- **Affects:** Market, Coins, Inventory, Ledger, Employment
- **Expected:** Validate, then commit. The usual checks (purse, stock, room), then the claims on both parties: goods the seller owes on an open contract (Contracts.held_for) and any reservation other than an offer never yield and refuse the sale; the seller's own asks for the item yield to it (a sale answers an ask); the buyer's own bids yield only to a purchase of the same item or a household necessity (food, firewood, medicine). The reservations of the offers a market settlement fills are exempt. Stale offers of both parties are first cut to what they hold. Then the yielding offers are cut (resize, or withdraw under their lot), then goods and coins move. A refused sale changes nothing; an accepted one cannot fail half way; no offer is ever left open without a reservation covering what remains (a fill above the bid's price cuts the rest to what the purse backs). Economy off: the sale is exactly as before.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/le03_claims_test.gd::test_ask_against_routine_sale`, `game/tests/le03_claims_test.gd::test_bid_against_routine_purchase`, `game/tests/le03_claims_test.gd::test_contract_holds_bind`, `game/tests/le03_claims_test.gd::test_same_goods_same_tick`, `game/tests/le03_claims_test.gd::test_same_coins_same_tick`, `game/tests/le03_claims_test.gd::test_rollback`, `game/tests/le03_claims_test.gd::test_rebook_to_backing`, `game/tests/le03_claims_test.gd::test_save_mid_reservation`, `game/tests/le03_claims_test.gd::test_determinism`, `game/tests/le02_market_test.gd::test_partial_and_duplicate_fills`
### INT-601 · Village paths that move goods for coins outside World.trade obey the same claims (Expansion 20 LE-03)
- **Trigger:** A villager buys at a premises' counter (`Business.sell`), from a caravan (smith's imports, the sage's medicine, seed, household and business food, stone, luxuries), or sells to the manor (repairs, granary, public works, tools, relief, mine supplies) or to a caravan (exports), while the economy is on.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/world.gd::claims_allow`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::claims_release`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::claims_free_coins`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::can_afford`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::purchase_qty`, `game/scripts/core/business.gd::sell`, `game/scripts/core/luxury.gd`, `game/scripts/core/mine_supply.gd::procure`, `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd`
- **Affects:** Market, Coins, Inventory, Treasury, Relief, Settlements
- **Expected:** The same rule as INT-600, checked before the path's own failure points and committed (own offers cut) just before the coins or goods move: coins a bid holds back are not spent on anything else (only a purchase of the bid's item or a necessity may take them, cutting the bid); goods a contract holds are not sold to the manor or a caravan; an ask yields to the sale. `can_afford` and `purchase_qty` count the coins no claim holds back. Economy off: every call returns at once and nothing differs (the caravan and courier paths stay as they are, parallel-work contract 1).
- **Tests:** `game/tests/le03_claims_test.gd::test_village_paths`, `game/tests/le03_claims_test.gd::test_bid_against_routine_purchase`, `game/tests/le03_claims_test.gd::test_switch_off`
### INT-602 · An escrow cannot take coins an offer holds back; a new contract cuts the parties' offers it leaves unbacked (Expansion 20 LE-03)
- **Trigger:** `Contracts.propose` / `fund_refusal` (any CON-01 contract with a villager buyer, npc_orders, the market's procurement) and `Contracts._open`, while the economy is on.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/contracts.gd::fund_refusal`, `game/scripts/core/contracts.gd::_open`, `game/scripts/core/contracts.gd::order_input`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_claims.gd::reconcile`
- **Affects:** Market, Coins, Employment
- **Expected:** A villager buyer sets aside only coins above its OD-19 reserve that no claim holds (the market's own procurement lifts its bid's reservation first, as in LE-02). When a contract opens, the seller's asks over goods now held for it and the buyer's bids over coins now in escrow are cut at once. A business with an open market bid for an input does not also order it by npc_orders (one channel per need). Economy off: unchanged.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/le03_claims_test.gd::test_escrow_and_orders`, `game/tests/contracts_test.gd`
### INT-603 · Units coming on a contract are not bought again; stale offers are cut at dawn (Expansion 20 LE-03)
- **Trigger:** `NPCBrain.wants` (any input purchase or need) while the market drives; the market's bids from needs and its reviews; `EconomyFoundation.dawn`.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::wants`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_claims.gd::inbound`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::open_need`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::need_for`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::post_bids`, `game/scripts/core/economy/economy_foundation.gd::dawn`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_claims.gd::reconcile_all`
- **Affects:** Market, Employment, Households, Save
- **Expected:** A business's wanted input, a tool, a housing material or goods owed are net of the units already coming to it on accepted or agreed (signing) contracts, so a bid that became a contract is not posted again and the routine purchase does not double the order (duplicate purchase). At dawn every owner with held reservations has its offers cut, newest first, to what it holds (goods or coins gone by tax, eating, crafting, theft, death): a stale offer can never fill. Market not driving or economy off: wants() and every dawn exactly as before.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/le03_claims_test.gd::test_inbound_no_duplicate`, `game/tests/le03_claims_test.gd::test_stale_offers`, `game/tests/le02_market_test.gd::test_switch`
### INT-604 · A household's budget and its food bid: obligations and necessities by their own urgency, then discretionary buying (Expansion 20 LE-03)
- **Trigger:** The market's dawn (INT-582: `post_bids`, `review`) while the economy is on and `market.drive` is true; the household's food need comes from `NPCBrain.evaluate_household` (hh.econ.days below reserve_days, OD-19).
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::budget_for`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::food_payer`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::food_item`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::food_units`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::need_for`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::post_bids`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::post_bid`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::review`
- **Affects:** Market, Food, Households, Coins
- **Expected:** The household's payer (its adult with the most coins; purses are not pooled) bids for the food it believes cheapest per food point, in units for the missing days; one food bid per household at a time. A villager's coins go first to the obligations and necessities it answers for (its household's food, goods it owes on a contract, its trade's tool), each by its own urgency, food from the whole purse (OD-19 necessities), the rest above the OD-19 reserve; a trade input or housing material bids with what is left, never past its own cost. Bids are sized and raised within that budget. A met need withdraws its bid. No food is created and no purchase is guaranteed; a household that cannot pay goes without (WDR-022).
- **Tests:** `game/tests/le03_economy_test.gd::test_food_bid`, `game/tests/le03_economy_test.gd::test_budget_order`, `game/tests/le02_market_test.gd::test_shortage_three_actors`
### INT-605 · Production honours accepted contracts; a worker waiting on a manor tool order may seek the tool elsewhere (Expansion 20 LE-03, gate "No tool-order traps")
- **Trigger:** A CON-01 goods contract opens with route "craft" (`Contracts._open`); a villager plans or works a recipe (`NPCBrain._do_recipe`, `World.work_craft`); a worker short of its trade's tool has a manor order open (EQ-19); while the economy is on and `market.drive` is true.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/contracts.gd::_open`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_claims.gd::on_contract_open`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_claims.gd::craft_ok`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_claims.gd::after_craft`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_contracts.gd::earmark`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_contracts.gd::sweep`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::work_craft`, `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::_do_recipe`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_needs.gd::derive`
- **Affects:** Crafting, Market, Employment, Inventory
- **Expected:** The seller earmarks the inputs it holds for the units still owed (design s.6), in its own pack. Earmarked inputs cannot be sold (INT-600) or worked into another product (the brain does not plan such a recipe; `work_craft` refuses it as "materials"); the contract's own recipe uses them and the earmark shrinks with the use. The earmark ends with the contract whatever ends it (deadline lapse, renegotiation, cancellation): the dawn sweep releases it, so nothing is held forever. A worker waiting on the manor's tool order still has its tool need and may bid for the tool and buy it from whoever sells; the forged tool then goes to the next worker short of one, or waits at the forge (EQ-19 unchanged). No tool or input is created. Market not driving or economy off: unchanged (no earmark, the LE-01 tool rule).
- **Tests:** `game/tests/le03_economy_test.gd::test_earmarked_inputs`, `game/tests/le03_economy_test.gd::test_no_tool_order_trap`, `game/tests/le03_economy_test.gd::test_tool_need_switch`
### INT-606 · The transaction authority: exchanges and shipments between village actors and settlement parties (Expansion 20 LE-03, X20-SETTLE-FOOD)
- **Trigger:** A caller (the settlement food trade, later regional links) calls `World.transactions.exchange`, `ship`, `deliver`, `fail` or `lose`; with or without the Living Economy switched on.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_transactions.gd::exchange`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_transactions.gd::ship`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_transactions.gd::_close`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_transactions.gd::_begin`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_transactions.gd::in_transit`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::trade`, `game/scripts/core/world.gd::claims_allow`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/settlement_party.gd::deal`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/settlement_party.gd::outside_coins`
- **Affects:** Market, Coins, Inventory, Settlements, Ledger, Save
- **Expected:** One validate-then-commit path (docs/expansion20/TRANSACTION_AUTHORITY.md): every check first (id, quantity, parties, stock, funds, room, claims while the economy is on), then goods and coins move together; a rejection changes nothing. A transaction id executes at most once (single-use per namespace; a replay returns the stored outcome). A shipment holds the seller's goods in custody and the buyer's coins in escrow until it is delivered (both pass), failed (both go back) or lost (a recorded sink, the buyer refunded); it changes state once. Village sides cross the village boundary as the caravan does (coin_sources, imported_/exported_); settlement sides are booked by SettlementParty. Coins and goods are conserved across the village, the settlements, transit and the sink; Regions.audit balances. Nothing calls it in shipped play yet: no change, no save key.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/le03_transactions_test.gd::test_ids_and_codes`, `game/tests/le03_transactions_test.gd::test_last_unit`, `game/tests/le03_transactions_test.gd::test_same_funds`, `game/tests/le03_transactions_test.gd::test_shipments`, `game/tests/le03_transactions_test.gd::test_interrupted_and_rejected`, `game/tests/le03_transactions_test.gd::test_save_load_replay`, `game/tests/le03_transactions_test.gd::test_days`, `game/tests/le03_transactions_test.gd::test_village_claims`, `game/tests/le03_transactions_test.gd::test_determinism`, `game/tests/le03_transactions_test.gd::test_switch`
### INT-607 · The manor, households, the chapel and tills are parties of the transaction authority (Expansion 20 LE-04)
- **Trigger:** `World.transactions.exchange` or `ship` naming `manor`, `hh:<n>`, `chapel` or a till ref.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_transactions.gd::_party`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_transactions.gd::_purse`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_transactions.gd::_add_coins`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_transactions.gd::exchange`
- **Affects:** Treasury, Granary, Households, Coins, Inventory, Ledger
- **Expected:** The manor pays from the treasury into the granary; a household sells from its pantry and is paid (or pays) through its head's own purse, never a pooled purse (OD-LE-07 D06); the chapel uses its fund and store; a till pays or receives coins only. Inside the village no coins or goods cross the boundary; with a settlement they do, as for a villager. Validation before any change, single-use ids, conserved.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/le04_test.gd::test_village_parties`, `game/tests/le03_transactions_test.gd::test_ids_and_codes`
### INT-608 · The manor buys or commissions workers' tools through the economy (Expansion 20 LE-04; EQ-19 under the Living Economy)
- **Trigger:** `NPCBrain._support_trades` (each evening's manor duties) while the economy is on and `market.drive` is true.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/npc_brain.gd::_support_trades`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::manor_tools`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::_hand_out_tools`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_market.gd::_sweep_tool_orders`, `game/scripts/core/contracts.gd::propose`, `game/scripts/core/contracts.gd::_pack`, `game/scripts/core/contracts.gd::plan`
- **Affects:** Treasury, Employment, Crafting, Market, Granary
- **Expected:** For a worker short of its trade's tool: a tool on sale is bought through the authority (treasury to the seller, the tool into the granary, then handed to the worker); else a smith is offered a CON-01 contract with the treasury's coins escrowed: the smith's own evaluation accepts, counters (met once within the band and the treasury floor) or refuses (the manor asks the next smith or the worker waits); the smith delivers to the reeve into the granary and is paid from escrow; an unmet commission lapses and the escrow returns. No advance is paid and no tool is created. Economy off or market not driving: EQ-19's advances exactly as before.

### INT-609 · A business's money and a dead party's advance move only by recorded transfers through the authority (Expansion 20 LE-04, OD-LE-07 D06, OD-LE-13B D12)
- **Trigger:** `BusinessMoney` moving coins between a till and its owner (necessity draw, owner pay, top-up, a till kept on handover, the owner's own contributions and distributions) while the market drives; a villager dying with a manor advance unmet (`Contracts.on_death`) while the market drives.

- **Affects:** Coins, Treasury, Households, Inheritance, Ledger
- **Expected:** Each till-owner move is a validated, journalled transfer (`coins:<purpose>`), neither earned nor spent; coins an offer holds back cannot be contributed. At a death, before inheritance: a paid-for good already made goes to its worker; else the manor's advance is paid back from the dead's own coins up to what it holds, the rest written off; the heirs' own purses untouched; nothing saved. Market not driving or economy off: unchanged.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/le04_test.gd::test_business_accounts`, `game/tests/le04_test.gd::test_estate_claims`
### INT-610 · Merchants speculate on what they know; manor wages may be bargained (Expansion 20 LE-04, OD-LE-11 D10, OD-LE-03 D02)

With the wage flag on, a manor wage agreed at taking lies between the existing wage and a capped ask by the worker's comfort; off: the existing wage.

### INT-611 · Economic knowledge is forgotten once no decision can act on it, and saved compactly (Expansion 20 LE-06)
- **Trigger:** `EconomyFoundation.dawn` (forgetting) and every save and load of the economy section, while the economy is on.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_knowledge.gd::forget_unusable`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_knowledge.gd::to_dict`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_knowledge.gd::load_dict`, `game/scripts/core/economy/economy_foundation.gd::dawn`

The knowledge section is written as rows over a string table (format 2, lossless); format 1 still loads. The economy's save stays bounded by recent dealings, not by time. Economy off: nothing.

### INT-612 · A delivered contract's price covers the seller's walk (Expansion 20 LE-05, design s.5 travel)

Economy off: CON-01's price unchanged.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/le05_test.gd::test_travel`

### INT-613 · The player's market journal: offers the player learned in person (Expansion 20 LE-05, OD-LE-14 D13)
