The host's player gets it by player_tell; a guest's is kept under its reference in the host's world (saved), no new network verb. Economy off: nothing.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/le05_test.gd::test_market_journal`, `game/tests/message_journal_test.gd`

### INT-620 · A settlement short of food buys wheat from another settlement: planned by the regional market, shipped through the transaction authority (Expansion 20 X20-SETTLE-FOOD, REG-BUY-D1)
- **Trigger:** `Regions.step_day` / `Regions.step_hour`, on the traders' wholesale day (every `road_trade_every_days`, after `road_trade`), when at least one settlement with a trader holds under three days of food for its people (food on the road included) and at least one other is not short, and the settlements live in a world (`World.transactions`).
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/settlement/regions.gd::food_round`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/regions.gd::ship_line`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/regions.gd::tx_ship`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/regions.gd::tx_in_transit`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/regions.gd::_shipments_audit`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/offmap.gd::food_release`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/offmap.gd::wants`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/offmap.gd::await_shipment`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/regional.gd::plan`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/regional.gd::ask_of`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/regional.gd::spendable`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/settlement_party.gd::sale_cap`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/settlement_party.gd::spend_limit`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_transactions.gd::ship`
- **Affects:** Settlements, Coins, Market, Households, Ledger, Save
- **Expected:** Buyers are the traders of short settlements (demand: the food shortfall in `food_trade_items`, wheat); sellers are the other settlements' traders (never food they imported) and their food businesses, each capped by its settlement's REG-BUY-P1 release (`food_release`); a short settlement sells none. `RegionalMarket.plan` picks the cheapest delivered offer at the seller's own floating price against the buyer's willingness; the buyer's spending is its till above its floor and half its trading capital; one self-fetched load a round. Each line becomes ONE call to the world's transaction authority (INT-606): `ship(settle_food/<place>:<seq>, buyer, seller, wheat, qty, total, due)` takes the seller's goods into transit custody and the buyer's price into escrow (outside lines on both settlements), together or not at all; a rejection (no_stock, no_funds, party_unavailable, ...) moves nothing and is recorded. The buyer's settlement records what it awaits (`transit`, saved); due day = 2 x the route's days, at least 1. Each settlement's audit balances through the outside lines; a whole-region count adds the settlements' part of the authority's `in_transit()` (`Regions.tx_in_transit`) as a holder (the LE-03 ledger rule); `Regions.audit` fails on an open settle_food shipment no settlement awaits, or an awaited one that is not open. No coin or good is created; nobody pays for anyone else; a settlement that cannot buy enough still goes hungry. Without a world (diagnostics) there is no authority and nothing is bought.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/settle_food_test.gd::test_purchase_and_books`, `game/tests/settle_food_test.gd::test_reserves_respected`, `game/tests/settle_food_test.gd::test_failures`, `game/tests/settle_food_test.gd::test_carriage_limit`, `game/tests/settle_food_test.gd::test_two_buyers_last_food`, `game/tests/settle_food_test.gd::test_competing_transfers`, `game/tests/settle_food_test.gd::test_repeated_trade_and_decline`, `game/tests/settle_food_test.gd::test_no_world_no_trade`, `game/tests/settle_food_test.gd::test_determinism`
### INT-621 · Food bought from another settlement is delivered once, at the due day's step, through the transaction authority (Expansion 20 X20-SETTLE-FOOD)
- **Trigger:** `OffMapSettlement.step_day`, before the morning market, for every awaited shipment due on or before that day.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/settlement/offmap.gd::_deliver`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/regions.gd::_arrive`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/regions.gd::tx_deliver`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/regions.gd::tx_fail`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/offmap.gd::_imported_left`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/offmap.gd::release_imported`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/offmap.gd::trader_has`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/offmap.gd::sell_to_village`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/offmap.gd::from_dict`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_transactions.gd::deliver`, `game/scripts/core/economy/econ_transactions.gd::fail`
- **Affects:** Settlements, Households, Market, Coins, Save
- **Expected:** `deliver(txid)`: the goods to the buyer's trader (offered to its households that morning, marked `imported`), the escrowed price to the seller, together. A shipment the authority cannot close (a party unavailable) stays awaited and open, its goods and coins in transit, and is asked again at the next step; once `food_lapse_days` late it is failed (goods back to the seller, price refunded) when both can take them. A shipment closed otherwise (delivered by a retry, failed) is dropped once: delivered counts once, a failed one is written in the settlement's record as never arrived. Phase-2 retries are duplicates (no second payment); a save between ship and delivery resumes with the same ids and holdings. Imported food is not offered to the caravan or another settlement until the place no longer wants food. Older saves (no `transit`, `food_seq`, `imported` or `food_*` flow keys) load with nothing on the road.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/settle_food_test.gd::test_delivery_once`, `game/tests/settle_food_test.gd::test_save_load_in_transit`, `game/tests/settle_food_test.gd::test_old_save_loads`, `game/tests/settle_food_test.gd::test_idempotent_replay`, `game/tests/settle_food_test.gd::test_interrupted_delivery`, `game/tests/settle_food_test.gd::test_abandoned_buyer`, `game/tests/settle_food_test.gd::test_imported_not_resold`
### INT-622 · The flight recorder records settlement food purchases, shortfalls and deliveries (Expansion 20 X20-SETTLE-FOOD, REC-DIAG)
- **Trigger:** `Regions.food_round` and `OffMapSettlement._deliver`, when the settlements live in a world whose recorder is on (`Regions.bind_world`).
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/settlement/regions.gd::_food_event`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/regions.gd::bind_world`, `game/scripts/core/recorder/world_recorder.gd::occurrence`
- **Affects:** Recorder, Settlements
- **Expected:** One `world/settlement/transaction` event per purchase (`settlement_food_purchase`, ok; refused lines `refused`), per shortfall (`settlement_food_unmet`, failed, reason buyer_short_of_coins, seller_lacks_item, refused_by_rules or pack_full) and per delivery (`settlement_food_delivery`: ok; interrupted with target_gone when no household was left (abandonment); failed with not_reached when the shipment was failed and refunded). Only listed codes; the recorder changes nothing in the game.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/settle_food_test.gd::test_recorder`
### INT-623 · Leaving and closing settle what the leavers own: a trader nobody works closes; a household's last leavers take its storefront till; a leaving market trader's stall takings are settled to them (Build 24 review, X20-SETTLE-FOOD worker)
- **Trigger:** `OffMapSettlement._develop` (weekly, `_close_idle`) for a settlement's trader with no worker; `Migration._pre_leave`, before any villager packs to leave for a settlement (household, work and marriage moves).
- **Source:** `game/scripts/core/settlement/offmap.gd::_close_idle`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/offmap.gd::_close_business`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/migration.gd::_take_own`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/migration.gd::_household_out`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/migration.gd::_work_out`, `game/scripts/core/settlement/migration.gd::_marry_out`, `game/scripts/core/stalls.gd::close`, `game/scripts/core/business_money.gd::keep_tills_of`
- **Affects:** Settlements, Coins, Market, Households, Treasury
- **Expected:** A settlement's trader closes by the same rule as every business once nobody has worked it for `switch_stay_days` (REG-X20-D1: no household is kept back): its till to the council, its stock to the granary (no self-purchase), any food it bought still on the road delivered first (the grower paid) so it follows the stock; with no trader the place sends no caravan and buys no food abroad. Before a villager leaves, every stall whose open session or seller is theirs is closed and settled to them (the manor's commission, the rest to their purse, which they carry), and when the leavers are everyone left in their household its storefront tills go to them (TILL-D1) and its pantry goes with them, for any move (a whole household, its last members, a lone last member leaving for work or marriage), trader or not (`Migration._take_own`, just before packing, after the move is certain). Coins conserved: nothing escheats to the manor that belonged to a living leaver.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/settle_review_test.gd::test_idle_trader_closes`, `game/tests/settle_review_test.gd::test_closing_trader_takes_in_shipments`, `game/tests/settle_review_test.gd::test_last_member_keeps_till`, `game/tests/settle_review_test.gd::test_leaving_trader_settles_stall`, `game/tests/settle_review_test.gd::test_every_business_closes_by_every_path`, `game/tests/settle_review_test.gd::test_lone_last_member_keeps_all`, `game/tests/settle_review_test.gd::test_non_trader_session_settled`

### INT-640 · A guest's requests are applied once each, in the order sent, under faults (Multiplayer Batch D)
- **Trigger:** A guest's request (`NetSession.request`, `send_verb`) arrives at the host (`c_request`): once, twice, late, ahead of an earlier one, or again after a reconnection.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::c_request`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::_enqueue`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::_release_held`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::_open_stream`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::_remember`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::apply_requests`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::s_reply`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::send_verb`
- **Affects:** Player state, Inventory, Coins, Ledger
- **Expected:** Each request carries a rising number; the guest tells the host its next number before its hello (`z_resume`). The host queues number `next` and any held numbers that follow it; a number above `next` waits up to `HOLD_SECONDS` (2 s) for the missing one, then goes in order; a number already applied or waiting is not applied again and, when its answer is kept (the last `REPLIES_KEPT`, 64, per identity), the answer is sent again. The guest takes one answer per number. The numbering is kept across a reconnection with the resume token and begun afresh on a new join. A request unanswered when a connection drops is not re-sent: the world sent at the rejoin is the truth. The same verb paths as before (INT-414, MP C) apply each request; nothing about what a verb does changes. A query verb (`container_pick`, `LOCAL_QUERIES`) is answered from the guest's copy and not sent.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/net_fault_test.gd::_repeats`, `game/tests/net_fault_test.gd::_lossy`, `game/tests/net_fault_test.gd::_host_killed`

### INT-641 · A guest's connection drops and comes back to the same character; the host takes it out of play meanwhile (Multiplayer Batch D, MP-D1, MP-D6)
- **Trigger:** The network between a guest and the host goes (ENet timeout, `TIMEOUT_MIN_MS`/`TIMEOUT_MAX_MS`, 4-10 s), a guest's Android app pauses and resumes (`Main._notification`), a guest process dies, or the host's process dies or is frozen.
- **Source:** `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::_lost`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::_begin_reconnect`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::_reconnect_step`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::on_app_resumed`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::admit`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::resumable`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::_drop_peer`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::c_hello`, `game/scripts/main.gd::net_interrupted`
- **Affects:** Player state, Save, Interface
- **Expected:** The guest's view stops (`net_interrupted`: windows closed, paused; nothing is simulated on a guest) and it retries the address that worked with backoff for `RECONNECT_SECONDS` (60 s; `HOST_PAUSED_SECONDS`, 300 s, when the host said it is paused), then goes to the title. On the host the old connection's character goes out of play (`away`, MP-D1) with every record kept and the world saved, as when a guest leaves. The guest comes back with its identity and the resume token given at its welcome (`z_token`, held in memory only): a new connection with the token takes the character back even before the host has found the old connection dead (the old one is dropped quietly); without it a second copy of an identity is still refused (`duplicate`). The rejoin sends the whole world as at a first join. A restarted host (its save) is rejoined the same way. No change to any game rule; no save key.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/net_fault_test.gd::_dropped`, `game/tests/net_fault_test.gd::_guest_pause`, `game/tests/net_fault_test.gd::_guest_killed`, `game/tests/net_fault_test.gd::_host_killed`, `game/tests/net_g1_test.gd`

### INT-642 · An Android host paused, a host stopping, and the invite code (Multiplayer Batch D, MP-D5, MP-D6, MP-INET)
- **Trigger:** The hosting app pauses or resumes (`NOTIFICATION_APPLICATION_PAUSED`/`RESUMED`), a frame of the hosted world comes after a freeze, the host stops hosting or quits (`NetSession.stop`), or the host opens its world from the window (Open to friends).
- **Source:** `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::on_app_paused`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::z_paused`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::stop`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::_drain`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::z_bye`, `game/scripts/main.gd::_process`, `game/scripts/main.gd::_notification`, `game/scripts/net/net_upnp.gd::start`, `game/scripts/net/net_invite.gd::make`, `game/scripts/net/net_invite.gd::parse`, `game/scripts/ui/net_window.gd::invite_code`, `game/scripts/ui/net_window.gd::reach_lines`, `game/scripts/net/net_session.gd::join_targets`
- **Affects:** Save, Calendar, Interface
- **Expected:** A paused host saves (as before) and tells its guests, who wait longer for it; a hosted world's frame longer than `HOST_STALL_SECONDS` (1 s) is simulated as one ordinary frame, so time stops while the host is away (the approved design: the world runs only while the host plays); single-player frames are unchanged. A host that stops sends `z_bye` and lets it out (`_drain`, up to 0.3 s) before closing, so its guests go to the title at once. Open to friends asks the router for the port on a thread (`NetUpnp`) and shows an invite code holding the LAN addresses, a global IPv6 address if any, and the public address only when the router mapped the port on a real public address; the window names what it can and cannot reach. A guest tries the code's addresses in order, each with its own limit (`NetInvite.ATTEMPT_SECONDS`), passes over an address answering with another world's tag, and says which kinds it tried when none answered. The mapping is removed when hosting stops.
- **Tests:** `game/tests/net_fault_test.gd::_codes`, `game/tests/net_fault_test.gd::_router`, `game/tests/net_fault_test.gd::_join_by_code`, `game/tests/net_fault_test.gd::_host_pause`, `game/tests/net_fault_test.gd::_host_stops`, `game/tests/net_ui_test.gd`
